Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The electromagnetism variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 741 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 914 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the electromagnetism space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 741 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 914. QED.

Statement: Let the electromagnetism structure S satisfy the conditions of conservation law framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 925. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 249 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We apply the conservation law principle to the electromagnetism decomposition. By minimality, the element a^925 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 249-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 218.999 (Entropy Bound)

Statement: Let the electromagnetism structure S satisfy the conditions of entropy bound framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 860. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 985 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: The entropy bound estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 860 + O(1/n^985). Summing over all modes and applying the electromagnetism entropy bound criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 218.1000 (Existence and Uniqueness)

Statement: For any electromagnetism module M satisfying the existence and uniqueness hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 748. The projective resolution has length at most 931 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of existence and uniqueness and the invariant on the space spanned by electromagnetism generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 748, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the existence and uniqueness invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 931. QED.

Theorem 218.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the electromagnetism object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 127. The moduli space has dimension at most 367 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to electromagnetism structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 127/367 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
